# Household hh_s0 — 28 days, Monday to Sunday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: flatmates. Rooms: balcony, bathroom, bedroom_1, bedroom_2, entry, kitchen, living.

Residents:

- **Yuki** (resident_1): works from home; very tidy (tidiness 0.78); average timing (jitter scale 0.94); often forgets pocket items; mood moderately affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.67); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: friends, gym, running. Chores they take on: dusting, fixing, grocery_run.
- **Nora** (resident_2): works outside the home; very tidy (tidiness 0.80); average timing (jitter scale 1.03); rarely forgets pocket items; mood moderately affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.51); sleeps in bedroom_2, works at the bedroom_2 desk. Hobbies: napping, reading_living, sketching. Chores they take on: dusting, grocery_run, ironing, vacuuming.

Pet: a dog, mainly looked after by Nora.

## Day 8 — Tuesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:deadline:resident_1:d7:due_today`: Yuki's deadline is at noon: a hurried morning, laptop and notebook never out of reach (06:30–12:00).
- `ep:deadline:resident_1:d7:relief`: Yuki made the deadline and is running on empty: things get left out, and the evening is TV (12:00–24:00).
- `ep:overslept:resident_1:d8`: Yuki overslept: a rushed morning with things dumped at the door, then a normal day (06:30–10:30).
- `ep:rough_day_at_work:resident_2:d8`: Nora had a rough day at work: goes quiet in the evening, keeps the phone close, the jacket lands on the couch (17:30–24:00).

Internal states (0–1): Yuki energy 0.82, hurriedness 0.61, distraction 0.51; Nora energy 0.56, hurriedness 0.21, distraction 0.28

## Day 11 — Friday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d11`: Yuki is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:02–15:32).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_2:d11`: Nora is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (10:11–12:11).
- `distracted:resident_1`: Yuki is distracted (0.80): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Nora has low energy (0.13): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

## Day 25 — Friday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d25`: Yuki is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (16:08–16:38).
- `ep:deadline:resident_1:d24:due_today`: Yuki's deadline is at noon: a hurried morning, laptop and notebook never out of reach (06:30–12:00).
- `ep:deadline:resident_1:d24:relief`: Yuki made the deadline and is running on empty: things get left out, and the evening is TV (12:00–24:00).
- `ep:overslept:resident_2:d25`: Nora overslept: a rushed morning with things dumped at the door, then a normal day (06:30–10:30).

Internal states (0–1): Yuki energy 0.57, hurriedness 0.55, distraction 0.30; Nora energy 0.30, hurriedness 0.32, distraction 0.40
